             * (XAxisKind, RollMethod) allowed-pair table
             * tenor-token grammar matches parent's x_axis_kind
             * discount-curve default & basis invariants
             * every active currency has at least one discount curve
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from riskquant.config.enums.asset_classes import (
    ASSET_SUBCLASS_TO_CLASS,
    VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
    VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
    AssetClass,
    AssetSubclass,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.discount_basis import (
    DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL,
    FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS,
    VALID_DISCOUNT_BASES,
    DiscountBasis,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.roll_methods import (
    ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS,
    VALID_ROLL_METHODS,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.unit_types import VALID_UNIT_SCALES, VALID_UNIT_TYPES
from riskquant.config.enums.x_axis_kinds import (
    VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
    X_AXIS_KINDS_REQUIRING_ROLL,
    X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX,
    XAxisKind,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.y_axis_kinds import VALID_Y_AXIS_KINDS
from riskquant.config.namespace import GlobalIdNamespace, enforce_id_namespace
from riskquant.exceptions import ConfigurationError
from riskquant.market_data.curves.extrap_methods import EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_methods import INTERP_METHOD_NAMES
from riskquant.market_data.risk_factors.shift_types import SHIFT_TYPE_NAMES

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

REQUIRED_SHEETS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "ccy",
        "holiday_calendars",
        "spot_roll_rule",
        "node_meta",
        "curve_meta",
        "curve_point_meta",
        "surface_meta",
        "surface_point_meta",
    }
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Generic helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _check_required_sheets(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    missing = REQUIRED_SHEETS - dict_of_dfs.keys()
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Missing required sheet(s): {sorted(missing)}.\n"
            f"Required: {sorted(REQUIRED_SHEETS)}"
        )


def _check_columns(df: pd.DataFrame, cols: frozenset[str], sheet_name: str) -> None:
    missing = cols - set(df.columns)
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Sheet {sheet_name!r} missing required columns: {sorted(missing)}.\n"
            f"Found: {sorted(df.columns)}"
        )


def _check_unique(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, sheet_name: str) -> None:
    if not df[col].is_unique:
        dupes = df[col][df[col].duplicated()].unique().tolist()
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Uniqueness violation: {sheet_name}.{col} must be unique. "
            f"Duplicates: {dupes}"
        )


def _check_composite_unique(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    cols: tuple[str, ...],
    sheet_name: str,
) -> None:
    dupes_mask = df.duplicated(subset=list(cols), keep=False)
    if dupes_mask.any():
        offending = df.loc[dupes_mask, list(cols)]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Composite uniqueness violation: "
            f"{sheet_name}({', '.join(cols)}) must be unique.\n"
            f"Duplicates:\n{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _check_enum_col(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    col: str,
    valid_set: frozenset[str],
    sheet_name: str,
    enum_name: str,
    *,
    nullable: bool,
) -> None:
    if col not in df.columns:
        return
    series = df[col]
    populated = series.dropna()
    if not nullable and len(populated) != len(series):
        n_null = int(series.isna().sum())
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Enum column {sheet_name}.{col} ({enum_name}) "
            f"contains {n_null} null value(s) but is non-nullable."
        )
    if populated.empty:
        return
    invalid = set(populated.astype(str)) - valid_set
    if invalid:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Enum violation in {sheet_name}.{col}: values not in {enum_name}: "
            f"{sorted(invalid)}.\nValid: {sorted(valid_set)}"
        )


def _check_fk(
    child_df: pd.DataFrame,
    child_col: str,
    parent_df: pd.DataFrame,
    parent_col: str,
    *,
    child_sheet: str,
    parent_sheet: str,
) -> None:
    child_vals = child_df[child_col].dropna().to_numpy()
    parent_vals = parent_df[parent_col].dropna().to_numpy()
    missing = np.setdiff1d(child_vals, parent_vals, assume_unique=False)
    if missing.size > 0:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"FK violation: {child_sheet}.{child_col} references values absent "
            f"from {parent_sheet}.{parent_col}.\nMissing: {missing.tolist()[:25]}"
        )


def _check_no_blank_strings(
    df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, sheet_name: str, *, nullable: bool
) -> None:
    if col not in df.columns:
        return
    series = df[col]
    if nullable:
        series = series.dropna()
    if series.empty:
        return
    is_blank = series.astype(str).str.strip() == ""
    if is_blank.any():
        idx = series.index[is_blank].tolist()[:10]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Blank string violation: {sheet_name}.{col} has empty values at "
            f"rows: {idx}"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 2 — per-sheet uniqueness
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    single_pk: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
        "ccy": ("ccy_name", "ccy_id"),
        "spot_roll_rule": ("rule_name",),
        "node_meta": ("node_id", "node_name"),
        "curve_meta": ("curve_id", "curve_name"),
        "curve_point_meta": ("rf_id",),
        "surface_meta": ("surface_id", "surface_name"),
        "surface_point_meta": ("rf_id",),
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in single_pk.items():
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet_name]
        for col in cols:
            _check_unique(df, col, sheet_name)

    _check_composite_unique(
        dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"],
        ("curve_id", "tenor_token"),
        "curve_point_meta",
    )
    _check_composite_unique(
        dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"],
        ("surface_id", "x_tenor_token", "y_label"),
        "surface_point_meta",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 3 — enum membership
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_enums(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    interp_names = frozenset(INTERP_METHOD_NAMES)
    extrap_names = frozenset(EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES)
    shift_names = frozenset(SHIFT_TYPE_NAMES)

    # --- node_meta ---
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "node_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "node_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df, "unit_type", VALID_UNIT_TYPES, "node_meta", "UnitType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "node_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "node_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )

    # --- curve_meta ---
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "curve_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "curve_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "unit_type",
        VALID_UNIT_TYPES,
        "curve_meta",
        "UnitType",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "curve_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "curve_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_axis_kind",
        VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
        "curve_meta",
        "XAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "curve_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "curve_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "discount_basis",
        VALID_DISCOUNT_BASES,
        "curve_meta",
        "DiscountBasis",
        nullable=False,
    )

    # --- surface_meta ---
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "surface_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "surface_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "unit_type",
        VALID_UNIT_TYPES,
        "surface_meta",
        "UnitType",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "surface_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "surface_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_axis_kind",
        VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
        "surface_meta",
        "XAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_axis_kind",
        VALID_Y_AXIS_KINDS,
        "surface_meta",
        "YAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )

    # --- spot_roll_rule ---
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]
    _check_enum_col(
        srr_df,
        "roll_method",
        VALID_ROLL_METHODS,
        "spot_roll_rule",
        "RollMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 4 — foreign keys
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_fks(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    ccy_df = dict_of_dfs["ccy"]
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    curve_pt_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    surf_pt_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]

    # ccy FKs
    _check_fk(
        node_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="node_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )
    _check_fk(
        curve_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="curve_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )
    _check_fk(
        surf_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="surface_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )

    # node_meta.fx_base_ccy is nullable; FK only on populated rows
    populated_fx_base = node_df.loc[node_df["fx_base_ccy"].notna(), ["fx_base_ccy"]]
    if not populated_fx_base.empty:
        _check_fk(
            populated_fx_base,
            "fx_base_ccy",
            ccy_df,
            "ccy_name",
            child_sheet="node_meta",
            parent_sheet="ccy",
        )

    # Curve / surface spot_roll_rule_name FKs (only populated rows)
    cm_pop = curve_df.loc[
        curve_df["spot_roll_rule_name"].notna(), ["spot_roll_rule_name"]
    ]
    if not cm_pop.empty:
        _check_fk(
            cm_pop,
            "spot_roll_rule_name",
            srr_df,
            "rule_name",
            child_sheet="curve_meta",
            parent_sheet="spot_roll_rule",
        )

    sm_pop = surf_df.loc[
        surf_df["spot_roll_rule_name"].notna(), ["spot_roll_rule_name"]
    ]
    if not sm_pop.empty:
        _check_fk(
            sm_pop,
            "spot_roll_rule_name",
            srr_df,
            "rule_name",
            child_sheet="surface_meta",
            parent_sheet="spot_roll_rule",
        )

    # Curve / surface point parent FKs
    _check_fk(
        curve_pt_df,
        "curve_id",
        curve_df,
        "curve_id",
        child_sheet="curve_point_meta",
        parent_sheet="curve_meta",
    )
    _check_fk(
        surf_pt_df,
        "surface_id",
        surf_df,
        "surface_id",
        child_sheet="surface_point_meta",
        parent_sheet="surface_meta",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 5 — bespoke domain invariants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_global_name_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """No name appears in more than one of {node, curve, surface} namespaces."""
    node_names = set(dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]["node_name"].astype(str))
    curve_names = set(dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]["curve_name"].astype(str))
    surface_names = set(dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]["surface_name"].astype(str))

    nc = node_names & curve_names
    ns = node_names & surface_names
    cs = curve_names & surface_names

    overlap = nc | ns | cs
    if overlap:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Name collision across container namespaces: {sorted(overlap)}.\n"
            f"Every node_name / curve_name / surface_name must be globally unique."
        )


def _validate_rf_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """rf_ids (node_id ∪ curve_point.rf_id ∪ surface_point.rf_id) must be globally unique."""
    node_ids = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]["node_id"].to_numpy()
    cp_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    sp_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()

    all_rf_ids = np.concatenate([node_ids, cp_ids, sp_ids])

    if np.unique(all_rf_ids).size != all_rf_ids.size:
        unique, counts = np.unique(all_rf_ids, return_counts=True)
        dupes = unique[counts > 1].tolist()
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"CRITICAL: rf_id collision across atomic namespaces: {dupes[:25]}.\n"
            f"node_id, curve_point.rf_id, and surface_point.rf_id share one ID space "
            f"and cannot overlap."
        )


def _validate_container_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """Ensure internal primary key uniqueness within container tables."""
    # Cross-container collisions are prevented by the namespace bounds.
    # We only need to check that the tables themselves don't have duplicate IDs.
    for table, col in [("curve_meta", "curve_id"), ("surface_meta", "surface_id")]:
        ids = dict_of_dfs[table][col].to_numpy()
        if np.unique(ids).size != ids.size:
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"CRITICAL: Duplicate primary keys found in {table}.{col}."
            )


def _validate_global_id_namespace(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """IDs that share a namespace must be globally bounded."""
    # 1. Gather ALL atomic Risk Factor IDs (Dimension = 0)
    node_ids = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"][
        "node_id"
    ].to_numpy()  # Node IDs are RF IDs by definition
    cp_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    sp_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    all_rf_ids = np.concatenate([node_ids, cp_ids, sp_ids])

    # 2. Gather Containers
    curve_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]["curve_id"].to_numpy()
    surface_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]["surface_id"].to_numpy()

    # 3. Enforce the mathematical bounds
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=all_rf_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.NODES)
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=curve_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.CURVES)
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=surface_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.SURFACES)


def _validate_asset_class_subclass_consistency(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """asset_subclass must map to the declared asset_class for all 3 meta tables."""

    def _expected_class(subclass_val: str) -> str | None:
        try:
            return ASSET_SUBCLASS_TO_CLASS[AssetSubclass(subclass_val)].value
        except ConfigurationError:
            return None

    for sheet in ("node_meta", "curve_meta", "surface_meta"):
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet]
        if "asset_class" not in df.columns or "asset_subclass" not in df.columns:
            continue
        valid = df[["asset_class", "asset_subclass"]].dropna()
        if valid.empty:
            continue
        expected = valid["asset_subclass"].map(_expected_class)
        bad_mask = expected.notna() & (valid["asset_class"] != expected)
        if bad_mask.any():
            offending = valid.loc[bad_mask].copy()
            offending["expected_class"] = expected[bad_mask]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"Domain violation: {sheet} has inconsistent "
                f"asset_class / asset_subclass.\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )


def _validate_fx_node_invariants(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    FX node contract:
      asset_class == "FX"  <=>  fx_base_ccy is non-null
      For FX nodes: node_name == fx_base_ccy + quote_ccy.
    """
    df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    is_fx = df["asset_class"] == AssetClass.FX.value
    has_base = df["fx_base_ccy"].notna()

    inconsistent = is_fx != has_base
    if inconsistent.any():
        offending = df.loc[
            inconsistent,
            ["node_name", "asset_class", "fx_base_ccy", "quote_ccy"],
        ]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Domain violation: asset_class=='FX' iff fx_base_ccy is non-null.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    fx_rows = df.loc[is_fx]
    if fx_rows.empty:
        return
    expected_names = fx_rows["fx_base_ccy"].astype(str) + fx_rows["quote_ccy"].astype(
        str
    )
    name_mismatch = fx_rows["node_name"] != expected_names
    if name_mismatch.any():
        offending = fx_rows.loc[
            name_mismatch, ["node_name", "fx_base_ccy", "quote_ccy"]
        ]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Domain violation: FX node_name must equal fx_base_ccy + quote_ccy.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # FX pairs are unique: (base, quote) appears at most once.
    composite = list(zip(fx_rows["fx_base_ccy"], fx_rows["quote_ccy"], strict=True))
    if len(set(composite)) != len(composite):
        from collections import Counter

        dupes = [k for k, c in Counter(composite).items() if c > 1]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"FX pair duplication: (base, quote) pairs appear more than once: {dupes}"
        )


def _validate_axis_roll_pairs(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    (XAxisKind, RollMethod) compatibility:

      - LABEL curves must have spot_roll_rule_name == NULL.
      - OFFSET_* curves must have spot_roll_rule_name populated.
      - The resolved (XAxisKind, RollMethod) pair must be in the
        ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS table.

    Vectorized: a single pass over each meta table, no row loops.
    """
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]
    rule_to_method: dict[str, str] = dict(
        zip(
            srr_df["rule_name"].astype(str),
            srr_df["roll_method"].astype(str),
            strict=True,
        )
    )

    # Pre-compute the allowed-pair set as plain strings (cheap O(K) build).
    allowed_pair_names: frozenset[tuple[str, str]] = frozenset(
        (xk.name, rm.name) for xk, rm in ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS
    )

    for sheet, name_col in (
        ("curve_meta", "curve_name"),
        ("surface_meta", "surface_name"),
    ):
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet]
        if df.empty:
            continue

        x_kind = df["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
        rule = df["spot_roll_rule_name"]
        rule_populated = rule.notna()
        needs_roll = x_kind.isin(X_AXIS_KINDS_REQUIRING_ROLL)

        # Missing rule on OFFSET container.
        missing = needs_roll & ~rule_populated
        if missing.any():
            offending = df.loc[missing, [name_col, "x_axis_kind"]]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: OFFSET containers missing spot_roll_rule_name:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        # Populated rule on LABEL container.
        extraneous = ~needs_roll & rule_populated
        if extraneous.any():
            offending = df.loc[
                extraneous, [name_col, "x_axis_kind", "spot_roll_rule_name"]
            ]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: LABEL containers with populated spot_roll_rule_name:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        # For OFFSET rows, check the (x_kind, method) pair is allowed.
        offset_rows = df.loc[needs_roll].copy()
        if offset_rows.empty:
            continue
        # method = offset_rows["spot_roll_rule_name"].astype(str).map(rule_to_method)
        # pair_strs = list(
        #     zip(offset_rows["x_axis_kind"].astype(str), method, strict=True)
        # )
        # bad_mask = np.array(
        #     [p not in allowed_pair_names for p in pair_strs], dtype=bool
        # )
        method = offset_rows["spot_roll_rule_name"].astype(str).map(rule_to_method)
        # Vectorized pair check: encode each (x_kind, roll_method) tuple as a
        # single "X|M" string and isin() against the precomputed allowed set.
        # Drops the Python-level list comprehension in the validation hot path.
        joined = offset_rows["x_axis_kind"].astype(str) + "|" + method.astype(str)
        allowed_join = frozenset(f"{xk}|{rm}" for xk, rm in allowed_pair_names)
        bad_mask = ~joined.isin(allowed_join).to_numpy(copy=False)
        if bad_mask.any():
            offending = offset_rows.loc[
                bad_mask, [name_col, "x_axis_kind", "spot_roll_rule_name"]
            ].copy()
            offending["roll_method"] = method[bad_mask].values
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: disallowed (x_axis_kind, roll_method) combinations:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )


def _validate_fh_bh_mmdd(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    fh_bh_mmdd = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]["fh_bh_mmdd"]
    if fh_bh_mmdd.empty:
        return
    fh_bh_mmdd = fh_bh_mmdd.dropna().astype(str)

    # Assert correct length of 9 (e.g., 0401|1001)
    bad_length = fh_bh_mmdd.str.len() != 9
    if bad_length.any():
        offending = fh_bh_mmdd[bad_length]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Invalid fh_bh_mmdd length:\n{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Assert correct format of 4 digits, pipe, 4 digits.
    bad_format = ~fh_bh_mmdd.str.match(r"^\d{4}\|\d{4}$")
    if bad_format.any():
        offending = fh_bh_mmdd[bad_format]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Invalid fh_bh_mmdd format (expected 'MMDD|MMDD'):\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_curve_point_token_grammar(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """
    Every curve_point.tenor_token matches its parent's x_axis_kind regex.

    Vectorized: group rows by parent x_axis_kind, apply pd.Series.str.match
    with the kind-specific regex to each group in one vectorized call.
    """
    cm_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    cp_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]
    if cp_df.empty:
        return

    # Join parent x_axis_kind onto each point row via map (vectorized).
    parent_kind_by_id = cm_df.set_index("curve_id")["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
    work = cp_df[["curve_id", "rf_id", "tenor_token"]].copy()
    work["x_axis_kind"] = work["curve_id"].map(parent_kind_by_id)

    bad_pieces: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    for kind_name, group in work.groupby("x_axis_kind"):
        # Explicitly cast kind_name to a string
        kind_name_str = str(kind_name)
        regex = X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX[XAxisKind[kind_name_str]]
        good = group["tenor_token"].astype(str).str.match(regex)
        bad = ~good
        if bad.any():
            bad_pieces.append(group.loc[bad])

    if bad_pieces:
        offending = pd.concat(bad_pieces, ignore_index=True)
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Curve point token grammar violations:\n"
            f"{offending.head(25).to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_surface_point_token_grammar(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """
    Every surface_point.x_tenor_token matches its parent's x_axis_kind regex.

    Vectorized: same per-kind-group str.match pattern as the curve-point
    validator.
    """
    sm_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    sp_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]
    if sp_df.empty:
        return

    parent_kind_by_id = sm_df.set_index("surface_id")["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
    work = sp_df[["surface_id", "rf_id", "x_tenor_token"]].copy()
    work["x_axis_kind"] = work["surface_id"].map(parent_kind_by_id)

    bad_pieces: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    for kind_name, group in work.groupby("x_axis_kind"):
        # Explicitly cast kind_name to a string
        kind_name_str = str(kind_name)
        regex = X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX[XAxisKind[kind_name_str]]
        good = group["x_tenor_token"].astype(str).str.match(regex)
        bad = ~good
        if bad.any():
            bad_pieces.append(group.loc[bad])

    if bad_pieces:
        offending = pd.concat(bad_pieces, ignore_index=True)
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Surface point token grammar violations:\n"
            f"{offending.head(25).to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_discount_invariants(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Discount-curve rules:
      - is_default_discount_for_ccy=True curves must have a real basis
        (not NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE).
      - Non-discount curves (basis=NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE) cannot be default.
      - Discount curves must have asset_class=='IR'.
      - The default's basis must equal FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.
      - Exactly one default per currency that has any discount curve.
      - (ccy, basis) is unique among discount curves.
      - Every currency in the ccy table that appears as a quote_ccy on
        any other container must have at least one discount curve.
    """
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    # ccy_df = dict_of_dfs["ccy"]

    is_disc = curve_df["discount_basis"] != DiscountBasis.NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE.name
    is_default = curve_df["is_default_discount_for_ccy"].astype(bool)

    # Default => is a real discount curve.
    bad = is_default & ~is_disc
    if bad.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad, ["curve_name", "discount_basis"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Default discount flag set on non-discount curve(s):\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Discount curves must be IR.
    bad_class = is_disc & (curve_df["asset_class"] != AssetClass.IR.value)
    if bad_class.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad_class, ["curve_name", "asset_class"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Discount curves must have asset_class=='IR'.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Default basis must equal the firm standard.
    bad_basis = is_default & (
        curve_df["discount_basis"] != FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.name
    )
    if bad_basis.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad_basis, ["curve_name", "discount_basis"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Default discount curves must use the firm-standard basis "
            f"{FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.name!r}.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Exactly one default per currency that has any discount curve.
    disc_curves = curve_df.loc[
        is_disc,
        ["curve_name", "quote_ccy", "discount_basis", "is_default_discount_for_ccy"],
    ]
    default_counts = (
        disc_curves.groupby("quote_ccy")["is_default_discount_for_ccy"]
        .sum()
        .astype(int)
    )
    bad_ccys = default_counts[default_counts != 1]
    if not bad_ccys.empty:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Each currency with a discount curve must flag exactly one as "
            f"default. Currencies with !=1 default:\n"
            f"{bad_ccys.to_string()}"
        )

    # (ccy, basis) is unique among discount curves.
    composite = disc_curves[["quote_ccy", "discount_basis"]]
    dupe_mask = composite.duplicated(keep=False)
    if dupe_mask.any():
        offending = disc_curves.loc[dupe_mask]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Duplicate (quote_ccy, discount_basis) among discount curves:\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Coverage: every currency that's a quote_ccy somewhere must have a
    # discount curve. (Skips currencies that exist in `ccy` but aren't used.)
    quote_ccys_used = pd.Index(
        pd.concat([curve_df["quote_ccy"], surf_df["quote_ccy"], node_df["quote_ccy"]])
        .dropna()
        .unique()
    )
    ccys_with_discount = set(disc_curves["quote_ccy"].astype(str))
    missing = sorted(set(quote_ccys_used.astype(str)) - ccys_with_discount)
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Currencies with no discount curve: {missing}. "
            f"Every active quote_ccy must have at least one discount curve."
        )

    # Sanity: discount_basis used by discount curves must be one of the real bases.
    real_bases = set(disc_curves["discount_basis"].astype(str))
    bogus = real_bases - DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL
    if bogus:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Unexpected discount_basis on discount curves: {sorted(bogus)}. "
            f"Allowed: {sorted(DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL)}"
        )


def _validate_no_blanks(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """Hard fail on empty / whitespace-only strings in non-nullable string cols."""
    string_cols_by_sheet: dict[str, dict[str, bool]] = {
        "ccy": {"ccy_name": False},
        "node_meta": {
            "node_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "unit_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "fx_base_ccy": True,
        },
        "curve_meta": {
            "curve_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "unit_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "x_axis_kind": False,
            "x_interp_method": False,
            "x_extrap_method": False,
            "spot_roll_rule_name": True,
            "discount_basis": False,
        },
        "curve_point_meta": {"tenor_token": False},
        "surface_meta": {
            "surface_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "unit_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "x_axis_kind": False,
            "x_interp_method": False,
            "x_extrap_method": False,
            "spot_roll_rule_name": True,
            "y_axis_kind": False,
            "y_interp_method": False,
            "y_extrap_method": False,
        },
        "surface_point_meta": {"x_tenor_token": False, "y_label": False},
        "spot_roll_rule": {
            "rule_name": False,
            "roll_method": False,
            "fh_bh_mmdd": True,
        },
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in string_cols_by_sheet.items():
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet_name]
        for col, nullable in cols.items():
            _check_no_blank_strings(df, col, sheet_name, nullable=nullable)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def validate_static_config_dfs(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Execute all validation tiers against the loaded config DataFrames.

    Raises ConfigurationError on the first violation with a descriptive message.
    """
    _check_required_sheets(dict_of_dfs)

    expected_columns: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
        "ccy": frozenset({"ccy_name", "ccy_id"}),
        "holiday_calendars": frozenset({"holiday_calendar_name", "holiday_date"}),
        "spot_roll_rule": frozenset(
            {
                "rule_name",
                "roll_method",
                "offset_in_tenor_units",
                "roll_month",
                "roll_day",
                "fh_bh_mmdd",
                "post_roll_shift",
            }
        ),
        "node_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "node_id",
                "node_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "unit_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "fx_base_ccy",
            }
        ),
        "curve_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "curve_id",
                "curve_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "unit_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "x_axis_kind",
                "x_interp_method",
                "x_extrap_method",
                "spot_roll_rule_name",
                "discount_basis",
                "is_default_discount_for_ccy",
            }
        ),
        "curve_point_meta": frozenset({"rf_id", "curve_id", "tenor_token"}),
        "surface_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "surface_id",
                "surface_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "unit_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "x_axis_kind",
                "x_interp_method",
                "x_extrap_method",
                "spot_roll_rule_name",
                "y_axis_kind",
                "y_interp_method",
                "y_extrap_method",
            }
        ),
        "surface_point_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "rf_id",
                "surface_id",
                "x_tenor_token",
                "y_label",
            }
        ),
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in expected_columns.items():
        _check_columns(dict_of_dfs[sheet_name], cols, sheet_name)

    _validate_no_blanks(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_enums(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fks(dict_of_dfs)

    # Tier 5 — domain
    _validate_rf_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_container_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_global_id_namespace(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_asset_class_subclass_consistency(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fx_node_invariants(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_axis_roll_pairs(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_curve_point_token_grammar(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_surface_point_token_grammar(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_discount_invariants(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fh_bh_mmdd(dict_of_dfs)

# src/riskquant/constants.py

"""
Common constants used across the riskquant codebase.

This module separates:
- typed sentinel values used in numeric payloads / arrays
- general configuration constants used by the portfolio engine
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# Typed sentinel values for payload fields
# ==============================================================================

# Universal sentinel for "no valid ID".
# Used across instrument_ids, underlying_ids, volatility_ids, and discount_curve_ids.
NO_ID = np.int32(-999_999)

# Universal sentinel for missing dates (settlement, delivery, expiry).
# Using -1 allows for clean `date_ord >= 0` masking.
NO_DATE_ORD32 = np.int32(-99_999)

# Specific sentinel for FX: No conversion required (Underlying CCY == Settlement CCY)
NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED = np.int32(-1)

# Invalid UIX sentinel for any universe index (*_uix fields).
# Used exclusively in the Execution Engine (blocks/compilers), never in Ingest.
INVALID_UIX = np.int32(-999)

# NOT IMPLEMENTED sentinels for fields expected to be populated but aren't yet.
NOT_IMPLEMENTED_SENTINEL: np.int8 = np.int8(-99)

# ==============================================================================
# Option encoding
# ==============================================================================

# 1 for call options, -1 for put options, 0 for not-an-option.
# Allows for branchless payoff calculation: payoff * cp_mult
CALL_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(1)
PUT_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(-1)
NOT_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(0)

# ==============================================================================
# Date encoding
# ==============================================================================

DATE_EPOCH: date = date(1970, 1, 1)

# ==============================================================================
# Numeric configuration
# ==============================================================================

ROUND_DECIMALS_LEVEL = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_QUANTITY = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_STRIKE_PRICE = 8

POSITION_DUST_TOLERANCE = 1e-8
PD_DUST_TOLERANCE = 1e-10

# ============================================
# --- DEVELOPMENT-ONLY FALLBACK VALUES
# ============================================

FALLBACK_MOCK_VOLATILITY_DO_NOT_SHIP: float = 0.50

# ==============================================================================
# --- EWMA Volatility Calculation Constants
# ==============================================================================

EWMA_DECAY_FACTOR: float = 0.94
MAX_EWMA_VOL_RATIO: float = 10.0
MIN_EWMA_VARIANCE: float = 1e-12

# ==============================================================================
# --- Credit Risk Constants
# ==============================================================================

NO_PARENT_OBLIGOR: np.int64 = np.int64(-1)
NO_RATING_ID: np.int8 = np.int8(0)
NO_SECTOR_ID: np.int32 = np.int32(-1)
NO_COUNTRY_ID: np.int32 = np.int32(-1)

# ==============================================================================
# RANDOM SEED FOR TESTING
# ==============================================================================

RNG_SEED: int = 42

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\market_data\builder.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/market_data/builder.py
"""
Market-data matrix builder.

Sits on the producer side of the valuation contract. Takes raw
scenario-shocked levels plus the `UixMapper` describing the run's
global universe, and emits a frozen `MarketDataMatrix` whose every
axis is aligned with the per-trade `_uix` coordinates carried by the
compiled blocks.

Key responsibilities
--------------------
1. FX effective matrix. Reads `UixMapper.fx_effective_pairs` to learn
   which `(fx_uix, invert)` pairs are needed across the portfolio,
   then materialises `fx_spots_effective: (K, S)` from the raw
   `(F, S)` direct-quote scenario matrix. Inverted rows are resolved
   via vectorised `np.reciprocal(out=...)`; direct rows are a single
   block copy. The pricers see zero inversion math.

2. Discount-factor grid. Log-linear interpolation in time, flat
   extrapolation. Routed through the zero-allocation Numba kernel
   with the pre-allocated output buffer.

3. Price-curve grid. Same shape as discount, but interpolation
   method varies per curve and is dispatched via `CURVE_MATH_REGISTRY`
   on `(interp_method, extrap_method)` group masks.

4. Time-to-expiry grid. Act/365.25 day count from `as_of_date` to
   each unique portfolio date, floored at 1e-9 to keep `sqrt(tte)`
   well-defined for same-day expiries.

5. Vol surfaces. Time-slice interpolation onto the unique-date
   universe. Output layout is `[surface, expiry, scenario, pillar]`
   so the strike-axis interpolation in the option-curve kernel can
   walk a contiguous pillar run per (trade, scenario).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_defs import (
    interp_log_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
)
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_dispatch import CURVE_MATH_REGISTRY
from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix
from riskquant.market_data.surfaces.y_axes_types import (
    SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_DELTA_CALL,
)
from riskquant.utils.dates import date_to_ord32

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.engine import UixMapper

_ACT_365_25: float = 365.25
_TTE_FLOOR: float = 1e-9


# ==============================================================================
# FX EFFECTIVE
# ==============================================================================


def _build_fx_spots_effective(
    *,
    raw_fx_spots: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    fx_effective_pairs: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    num_scenarios: int,
) -> npt.NDArray[np.float64]:
    """
    Materialise the `(K, S)` effective FX matrix from the dedup pair table.

    `fx_effective_pairs[k] == (fx_uix, invert)` — `fx_uix` is a row index
    into `raw_fx_spots` (or `< 0` for an identity-row sentinel), `invert`
    is `0` for direct and `1` for inverted (i.e. `1 / rate`).
    """
    k_pairs = fx_effective_pairs.shape[0]
    out = np.empty((k_pairs, num_scenarios), dtype=np.float64)

    if k_pairs == 0:
        return out

    if fx_effective_pairs.shape[1] != 2:
        raise ValueError(
            f"fx_effective_pairs must have shape (K, 2); got {fx_effective_pairs.shape}"
        )

    fx_uixs = fx_effective_pairs[:, 0]
    invs = fx_effective_pairs[:, 1]

    sentinel_mask = fx_uixs < 0
    direct_mask = (~sentinel_mask) & (invs == 0)
    invert_mask = (~sentinel_mask) & (invs == 1)

    if sentinel_mask.any():
        out[sentinel_mask, :] = 1.0

    if direct_mask.any():
        np.copyto(out[direct_mask, :], raw_fx_spots[fx_uixs[direct_mask]])

    if invert_mask.any():
        np.reciprocal(
            raw_fx_spots[fx_uixs[invert_mask]],
            out=out[invert_mask, :],
        )

    return out


# ==============================================================================
# CURVE GRIDS
# ==============================================================================


def _build_price_curve_grid(
    *,
    raw_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_values: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    target_dates: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    interp_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    extrap_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    num_scenarios: int,
) -> npt.NDArray[np.float64]:
    """
    Group-dispatched 3-D curve interpolation onto the unique-date grid.

    Curves sharing the same `(interp_method, extrap_method)` pair are
    gathered into a contiguous scratch buffer, fed to the dispatched
    kernel, then scatter-assigned back into `out_grid`.

    Scatter contract
    ----------------
    `out_grid[idx]` on the LHS of an assignment is NumPy fancy-index
    setitem and is in-place. `out_grid[idx]` on the RHS (the kernel's
    original signature) would have been a fresh COPY — the kernel
    would have written into the copy, the copy would have been
    discarded, and `out_grid` would have been left as `np.empty()`
    garbage. The scratch + scatter pattern is the correct fix.
    """
    c_curves = raw_values.shape[0]
    d_dates = target_dates.shape[0]

    out_grid = np.empty((c_curves, d_dates, num_scenarios), dtype=np.float64)

    if d_dates == 0 or c_curves == 0:
        return out_grid

    for (interp_id, extrap_id), kernel in CURVE_MATH_REGISTRY.items():
        mask = (interp_methods == interp_id) & (extrap_methods == extrap_id)
        if not mask.any():
            continue

        idx = np.where(mask)[0]
        n_in_group = idx.shape[0]

        # Gather to a contiguous scratch buffer the kernel can write to.
        scratch = np.empty(
            (n_in_group, d_dates, num_scenarios),
            dtype=np.float64,
        )
        kernel(
            np.ascontiguousarray(raw_tenors[idx]),
            np.ascontiguousarray(raw_values[idx]),
            target_dates,
            scratch,
        )
        # Scatter back. Fancy-indexed ASSIGNMENT is in-place.
        out_grid[idx] = scratch

    return out_grid


def _build_discount_factor_grid(
    *,
    raw_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_ln_dfs: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    target_dates: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    num_scenarios: int,
) -> npt.NDArray[np.float64]:
    """
    Discount-factor grid: log-linear interpolation in time, flat extrap.

    Domain-convention: every discount curve in the system is quoted as
    log(DF) at pillar dates and interpolated linearly on log(DF), which
    is equivalent to linear interpolation on zero rates between pillars.
    The flat extrapolation prevents pathological exponentials at the long
    end and matches the front-office convention.
    """
    c_curves = raw_ln_dfs.shape[0]
    d_dates = target_dates.shape[0]

    out_grid = np.empty((c_curves, d_dates, num_scenarios), dtype=np.float64)

    if d_dates == 0 or c_curves == 0:
        return out_grid

    interp_log_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
        raw_tenors,
        raw_ln_dfs,
        target_dates,
        out_grid,
    )
    return out_grid


# ==============================================================================
# VOL SURFACES
# ==============================================================================


def _build_vol_surfaces(
    *,
    raw_vol_surface_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_vol_surface_strikes: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_vol_surface_values: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    surface_interp_x_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    surface_extrap_x_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    surface_y_axis_type_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    target_dates: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    num_scenarios: int,
) -> npt.NDArray[np.float64]:
    """
    Time-slice every vol surface onto the portfolio's unique-date axis
    and emit the canonical kernel-facing layout.

    Input  : `raw_vol_surface_values` is `(C_surf, M_tenors, K_strikes, S_scn)`.
    Output : `(C_surf, D_dates, S_scn, K_strikes)` — pillar in trailing axis.
    """
    if (surface_y_axis_type_ids == SURFACE_Y_AXIS_TYPE_DELTA_CALL).any():
        raise ValueError(
            "FATAL: Delta-quoted Volatility Surfaces detected in runtime builder. "
            "DELTA_CALL surfaces must be pre-processed and converted to STRIKE or "
            "MONEYNESS by the Calibration module before being passed to the "
            "MarketDataMatrix."
        )

    c_surf = raw_vol_surface_tenors.shape[0] if raw_vol_surface_tenors.ndim > 0 else 0
    k_strikes = (
        raw_vol_surface_strikes.shape[1] if raw_vol_surface_strikes.ndim > 1 else 0
    )
    d_dates = target_dates.shape[0]

    if d_dates == 0 or raw_vol_surface_values.size == 0 or c_surf == 0:
        return np.empty((c_surf, d_dates, num_scenarios, k_strikes), dtype=np.float64)

    c_in, m_tenors, k_in, s_in = raw_vol_surface_values.shape
    if c_in != c_surf or k_in != k_strikes or s_in != num_scenarios:
        raise ValueError(
            f"raw_vol_surface_values shape {raw_vol_surface_values.shape} is "
            f"inconsistent with tenors/strikes/scenarios "
            f"({c_surf}, {m_tenors}, {k_strikes}, {num_scenarios})."
        )

    # Step 1. Fold (pillar, scenario) into one trailing axis and feed
    # the time-interp kernels as a 3-D (c, m_tenors, k * s) array.
    flat_in = raw_vol_surface_values.reshape(
        c_surf, m_tenors, k_strikes * num_scenarios
    )
    time_sliced_flat = _build_price_curve_grid(
        raw_tenors=raw_vol_surface_tenors,
        raw_values=np.ascontiguousarray(flat_in),
        target_dates=target_dates,
        interp_methods=surface_interp_x_methods,
        extrap_methods=surface_extrap_x_methods,
        num_scenarios=k_strikes * num_scenarios,
    )

    # Step 2. Reshape back to (c, d, k, s) and swap to land in the kernel-
    # facing (c, d, s, k) layout. Force C-contiguous because the kernel
    # signature pins `[:, :, :, ::1]`.
    canonical = time_sliced_flat.reshape(
        c_surf, d_dates, k_strikes, num_scenarios
    ).swapaxes(2, 3)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(canonical)


# ==============================================================================
# TOP-LEVEL ENTRY POINT
# ==============================================================================


def build_market_data_matrix(
    *,
    as_of_date: date,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
    num_scenarios: int,
    # ---- Raw scalar matrices (F/P/Fwd/Vol, scenarios) ----
    raw_fx_spots: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_prices: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_forward_nodes: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_vol_nodes: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    # ---- Curve configs ----
    raw_discount_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_discount_ln_dfs: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_price_curve_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_price_curve_values: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    curve_interp_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    curve_extrap_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    # ---- Surface configs ----
    raw_vol_surface_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_vol_surface_strikes: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    raw_vol_surface_values: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    surface_interp_x_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    surface_extrap_x_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
    surface_y_axis_type_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int8],
) -> MarketDataMatrix:
    """
    Assemble a frozen `MarketDataMatrix` aligned to the run's UIX universes.

    Parameters
    ----------
    uix_mapper
        Source of truth for the FX-effective pair table and the unique-
        date axis. Built once per run by `build_uix_mapper`.

    Returns
    -------
    MarketDataMatrix
        Valuation-ready container; pricers consume directly.
    """
    unique_dates_ord32 = uix_mapper.date_ord32_universe
    d_dates = unique_dates_ord32.shape[0]

    # ---- 1. FX effective matrix ------------------------------------------
    fx_spots_effective = _build_fx_spots_effective(
        raw_fx_spots=raw_fx_spots,
        fx_effective_pairs=uix_mapper.fx_effective_pairs,
        num_scenarios=num_scenarios,
    )

    # ---- 2. Discount factor grid -----------------------------------------
    discount_factor_grid = _build_discount_factor_grid(
        raw_tenors=raw_discount_tenors,
        raw_ln_dfs=raw_discount_ln_dfs,
        target_dates=unique_dates_ord32,
        num_scenarios=num_scenarios,
    )

    # ---- 3. Price curve grid ---------------------------------------------
    price_curve_grid = _build_price_curve_grid(
        raw_tenors=raw_price_curve_tenors,
        raw_values=raw_price_curve_values,
        target_dates=unique_dates_ord32,
        interp_methods=curve_interp_methods,
        extrap_methods=curve_extrap_methods,
        num_scenarios=num_scenarios,
    )

    # ---- 4. TTE grid (act/365.25) ----------------------------------------
    as_of_ord32 = date_to_ord32(as_of_date)
    if d_dates == 0:
        tte_grid = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        tte_grid = np.maximum(
            (unique_dates_ord32.astype(np.float64) - float(as_of_ord32)) / _ACT_365_25,
            _TTE_FLOOR,
        )

    # ---- 5. Vol surfaces (canonical [c, d, s, k] layout) -----------------
    vol_surfaces = _build_vol_surfaces(
        raw_vol_surface_tenors=raw_vol_surface_tenors,
        raw_vol_surface_strikes=raw_vol_surface_strikes,
        raw_vol_surface_values=raw_vol_surface_values,
        surface_interp_x_methods=surface_interp_x_methods,
        surface_extrap_x_methods=surface_extrap_x_methods,
        surface_y_axis_type_ids=surface_y_axis_type_ids,
        target_dates=unique_dates_ord32,
        num_scenarios=num_scenarios,
    )

    return MarketDataMatrix(
        num_scenarios=num_scenarios,
        fx_spots_effective=fx_spots_effective,
        prices=raw_prices,
        forward_nodes=raw_forward_nodes,
        vol_nodes=raw_vol_nodes,
        discount_factor_grid=discount_factor_grid,
        price_curve_grid=price_curve_grid,
        tte_grid=tte_grid,
        vol_surface_strikes=raw_vol_surface_strikes,
        vol_surfaces=vol_surfaces,
    )
```

# src\riskquant\market_data\curves\extrap_methods.py

from __future__ import annotations

from enum import IntEnum

import numpy as np


class ExtrapMethod(IntEnum):
    FLAT = 1
    LINEAR = 2


# compiler-friendly int8 constants
EXTRAP_FLAT: np.int8 = np.int8(ExtrapMethod.FLAT)
EXTRAP_LINEAR: np.int8 = np.int8(ExtrapMethod.LINEAR)


# Extrap methods
EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(
    method.name.upper() for method in ExtrapMethod
)

# Central registry
EXTRAP_METHOD_REGISTRY: dict[str, ExtrapMethod] = {
    "LINEAR": ExtrapMethod.LINEAR,
    "FLAT": ExtrapMethod.FLAT,
}


def validate_extrapolation_methods_dict(
    extrapolation_methods_dict: dict[str, int],
) -> None:
    expected = {
        name: int(member) for name, member in ExtrapMethod.__members__.items()
    }
    got = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in extrapolation_methods_dict.items()}

    if got == expected:
        return

    missing = {k: expected[k] for k in expected.keys() - got.keys()}
    extra = {k: got[k] for k in got.keys() - expected.keys()}
    different = {
        k: {"expected": expected[k], "got": got[k]}
        for k in (expected.keys() & got.keys())
        if expected[k] != got[k]
    }

    raise ValueError(
        "extrapolation_methods sheet does not match riskquant ExtrapMethod enum.\n"
        f"missing={missing}\n"
        f"extra={extra}\n"
        f"different={different}\n"
        "Fix the Excel extrapolation_methods table or update riskquant.market_data.curves.extrap_methods.ExtrapMethod."
    )

```python
# src/riskquant/market_data/curves/interp_defs.py
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numba as nb
import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# --- LINEAR INTERPOLATION WITH FLAT EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_linear_flat_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Vectorized 3D Linear Interpolation with Flat Extrapolation.
    Shapes: tenors (c_curves, m_pillars), values (c_curves, m_pillars, s_scenarios), targets (d_targets,)
    Returns: (c_curves, d_targets, s_scenarios)
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    idx1 = np.clip(
        np.sum(tenors[:, None, :] <= targets[None, :, None], axis=2),
        1,
        m_pillars - 1,
    )
    idx0 = idx1 - 1

    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    t0, t1 = tenors[c_idx, idx0], tenors[c_idx, idx1]
    y0, y1 = values[c_idx, idx0, :], values[c_idx, idx1, :]

    dt = np.where((t1 - t0) == 0, 1.0, t1 - t0)
    w = (targets[None, :] - t0) / dt

    # FLAT EXTRAPOLATION: Clamp weights to [0.0, 1.0]
    w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 1.0)

    return y0 + w[:, :, None] * (y1 - y0)


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """Zero-allocation 3D Linear Interpolator with Flat Extrapolation."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]

            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="right")
            idx1 = min(max(idx, 1), m_pillars - 1)
            idx0 = idx1 - 1

            t0 = tenors[c, idx0]
            t1 = tenors[c, idx1]

            dt = t1 - t0
            if dt == 0.0:
                dt = 1.0

            w = (target - t0) / dt
            w = min(max(w, 0.0), 1.0)

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                y0 = values[c, idx0, s]
                y1 = values[c, idx1, s]
                out[c, d, s] = y0 + w * (y1 - y0)


# ==============================================================================
# --- LINEAR INTERPOLATION WITH LINEAR EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_linear_linear_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Vectorized 3D Linear Interpolation with Linear Extrapolation.
    Shapes: tenors (c_curves, m_pillars), values (c_curves, m_pillars, s_scenarios), targets (d_targets,)
    Returns: (c_curves, d_targets, s_scenarios)
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    idx1 = np.clip(
        np.sum(tenors[:, None, :] <= targets[None, :, None], axis=2),
        1,
        m_pillars - 1,
    )
    idx0 = idx1 - 1

    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    t0, t1 = tenors[c_idx, idx0], tenors[c_idx, idx1]
    y0, y1 = values[c_idx, idx0, :], values[c_idx, idx1, :]

    dt = np.where((t1 - t0) == 0, 1.0, t1 - t0)
    w = (targets[None, :] - t0) / dt

    # LINEAR EXTRAPOLATION: Do not clamp weights
    return y0 + w[:, :, None] * (y1 - y0)


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_linear_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """Zero-allocation 3D Linear Interpolator with Linear Extrapolation."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]

            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="right")
            idx1 = min(max(idx, 1), m_pillars - 1)
            idx0 = idx1 - 1

            t0 = tenors[c, idx0]
            t1 = tenors[c, idx1]

            dt = t1 - t0
            if dt == 0.0:
                dt = 1.0

            w = (target - t0) / dt
            # LINEAR EXTRAPOLATION: Deliberately do not clamp w

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                y0 = values[c, idx0, s]
                y1 = values[c, idx1, s]
                out[c, d, s] = y0 + w * (y1 - y0)


# ==============================================================================
# --- LOG-LINEAR INTERPOLATION WITH FLAT EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_log_linear_flat_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Vectorized 3D Log-Linear Interpolation with Constant Forward Extrapolation.
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    mask = tenors[:, None, :] <= targets[None, :, None]
    idx1 = np.sum(mask, axis=2)
    idx1 = np.clip(idx1, 1, m_pillars - 1)
    idx0 = idx1 - 1

    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    t0 = tenors[c_idx, idx0]
    t1 = tenors[c_idx, idx1]

    y0 = values[c_idx, idx0, :]
    y1 = values[c_idx, idx1, :]

    dt = t1 - t0
    dt = np.where(dt == 0, 1.0, dt)
    w = (targets[None, :] - t0) / dt

    ln_interp = y0 + w[:, :, None] * (y1 - y0)
    return np.exp(ln_interp)


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_log_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """
    Zero-allocation 3D Log-Linear Interpolator.
    Mathematically equivalent to linear interpolation on Zero Rates.
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]

            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="right")
            idx1 = min(max(idx, 1), m_pillars - 1)
            idx0 = idx1 - 1

            t0 = tenors[c, idx0]
            t1 = tenors[c, idx1]

            dt = t1 - t0
            if dt == 0.0:
                dt = 1.0

            w = (target - t0) / dt

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                y0 = values[c, idx0, s]
                y1 = values[c, idx1, s]
                ln_interp = y0 + w * (y1 - y0)
                out[c, d, s] = math.exp(ln_interp)


# ==============================================================================
# --- LOG-LINEAR INTERPOLATION WITH LINEAR EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_log_linear_linear_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Vectorized 3D Log-Linear Interpolation with Linear Extrapolation.
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    idx1 = np.clip(
        np.sum(tenors[:, None, :] <= targets[None, :, None], axis=2),
        1,
        m_pillars - 1,
    )
    idx0 = idx1 - 1

    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    t0, t1 = tenors[c_idx, idx0], tenors[c_idx, idx1]
    y0, y1 = values[c_idx, idx0, :], values[c_idx, idx1, :]

    dt = np.where((t1 - t0) == 0, 1.0, t1 - t0)
    w = (targets[None, :] - t0) / dt

    ln_interp = y0 + w[:, :, None] * (y1 - y0)
    return np.exp(ln_interp)


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_log_linear_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """
    Zero-allocation 3D Log-Linear Interpolator with Linear Extrapolation.
    Mathematically identical to the flat forward logic in log-space.
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]

            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="right")
            idx1 = min(max(idx, 1), m_pillars - 1)
            idx0 = idx1 - 1

            t0 = tenors[c, idx0]
            t1 = tenors[c, idx1]

            dt = t1 - t0
            if dt == 0.0:
                dt = 1.0

            w = (target - t0) / dt

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                y0 = values[c, idx0, s]
                y1 = values[c, idx1, s]
                ln_interp = y0 + w * (y1 - y0)
                out[c, d, s] = math.exp(ln_interp)


# ==============================================================================
# --- STEP LEFT (LOCF) INTERPOLATION WITH FLAT EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_step_left_flat_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Vectorized 3D Step Left (LOCF) Interpolation."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    idx = np.clip(
        np.sum(tenors[:, None, :] <= targets[None, :, None], axis=2) - 1,
        0,
        m_pillars - 1,
    )
    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    return values[c_idx, idx, :]


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_step_left_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """Zero-allocation 3D Step Left (LOCF) Interpolator."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]
            # side="right" ensures we find the first index > target.
            # Subtracting 1 gives the index <= target (Last Observation Carried Forward).
            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="right") - 1
            idx = min(max(idx, 0), m_pillars - 1)

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                out[c, d, s] = values[c, idx, s]


# ==============================================================================
# --- STEP LEFT (LOCF) INTERPOLATION WITH LINEAR EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_step_left_linear_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Vectorized 3D Step Left (LOCF) Interpolation with Linear Extrapolation."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    idx = np.clip(
        np.sum(tenors[:, None, :] <= targets[None, :, None], axis=2) - 1,
        0,
        m_pillars - 1,
    )
    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    return values[c_idx, idx, :]


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_step_left_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """
    Zero-allocation 3D Step Left (LOCF) Interpolator.
    Mathematically identical to flat extrapolation for step functions.
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]
            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="right") - 1
            idx = min(max(idx, 0), m_pillars - 1)

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                out[c, d, s] = values[c, idx, s]


# ==============================================================================
# --- STEP RIGHT (NOCB) INTERPOLATION WITH FLAT EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_step_right_flat_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Vectorized 3D Step Right (NOCB) Interpolation."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    idx = np.clip(
        np.sum(tenors[:, None, :] < targets[None, :, None], axis=2),
        0,
        m_pillars - 1,
    )
    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    return values[c_idx, idx, :]


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_step_right_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """Zero-allocation 3D Step Right (NOCB) Interpolator."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]
            # side="left" ensures we find the first index >= target (Next Observation Carried Backward).
            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="left")
            idx = min(max(idx, 0), m_pillars - 1)

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                out[c, d, s] = values[c, idx, s]


# ==============================================================================
# --- STEP RIGHT (NOCB) INTERPOLATION WITH LINEAR EXTRAPOLATION ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_step_right_linear_extrap_3d(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Vectorized 3D Step Right (NOCB) Interpolation with Linear Extrapolation."""
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    idx = np.clip(
        np.sum(tenors[:, None, :] < targets[None, :, None], axis=2),
        0,
        m_pillars - 1,
    )
    c_idx = np.arange(c_curves)[:, None]
    return values[c_idx, idx, :]


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_step_right_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel(
    tenors: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """
    Zero-allocation 3D Step Right (NOCB) Interpolator.
    Mathematically identical to flat extrapolation for step functions.
    """
    c_curves, m_pillars = tenors.shape
    d_target_dates = targets.shape[0]
    s_scenarios = values.shape[2]

    for c in nb.prange(c_curves):
        for d in range(d_target_dates):
            target = targets[d]
            idx = np.searchsorted(tenors[c], target, side="left")
            idx = min(max(idx, 0), m_pillars - 1)

            for s in range(s_scenarios):
                out[c, d, s] = values[c, idx, s]


# ==============================================================================
# --- 2D SURFACE STRIKE INTERPOLATOR ---
# ==============================================================================


def interp_strike_linear_flat_extrap(
    pillars: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N_trades, K_strikes)
    values: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N_trades, K_strikes, S_scenarios)
    targets: np.ndarray,  # Shape: (N_trades, S_scenarios)
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Vectorized 1D linear interpolation along the strike/Y-axis with flat extrapolation.
    Used dynamically inside Option Pricers to resolve exact strike vol across scenarios.
    """
    N, K_dim = pillars.shape
    S = targets.shape[1]

    mask = pillars[:, None, :] <= targets[:, :, None]
    idx1 = np.sum(mask, axis=2)
    idx1 = np.clip(idx1, 1, K_dim - 1)
    idx0 = idx1 - 1

    n_idx = np.arange(N)[:, None]
    s_idx = np.arange(S)[None, :]

    x0 = pillars[n_idx, idx0]
    x1 = pillars[n_idx, idx1]

    y0 = values[n_idx, idx0, s_idx]
    y1 = values[n_idx, idx1, s_idx]

    dx = np.where((x1 - x0) == 0, 1.0, x1 - x0)
    w = (targets - x0) / dx

    w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y0 + w * (y1 - y0)


@nb.njit(
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)
def interp_strike_linear_flat_extrap_numba_kernel(
    pillars: np.ndarray,
    values: np.ndarray,
    targets: np.ndarray,
    out: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """
    Zero-allocation 2D Surface Strike Interpolator.
    Pre-allocates an (N_trades, S_scenarios) array and resolves exactly.
    """
    n_trades, k_strikes = pillars.shape
    s_scenarios = targets.shape[1]

    for n in nb.prange(n_trades):
        for s in range(s_scenarios):
            target = targets[n, s]

            idx = np.searchsorted(pillars[n], target, side="right")
            idx1 = min(max(idx, 1), k_strikes - 1)
            idx0 = idx1 - 1

            x0 = pillars[n, idx0]
            x1 = pillars[n, idx1]

            dx = x1 - x0
            if dx == 0.0:
                dx = 1.0

            w = (target - x0) / dx
            w = min(max(w, 0.0), 1.0)

            y0 = values[n, idx0, s]
            y1 = values[n, idx1, s]

            out[n, s] = y0 + w * (y1 - y0)
```

# src/riskquant/market_data/curves/interp_dispatch.py
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable

import numpy as np

from riskquant.market_data.curves.extrap_methods import (
    EXTRAP_FLAT,
    EXTRAP_LINEAR,
)

# Import the fused Numba kernels
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_defs import (
    interp_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    interp_linear_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    interp_log_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    interp_log_linear_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    interp_step_left_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    interp_step_left_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    interp_step_right_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    interp_step_right_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
)

# Import your enum constants
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_methods import (
    INTERP_LINEAR,
    INTERP_LOG_LINEAR,
    INTERP_STEP_LEFT,
    INTERP_STEP_RIGHT,
)

# ============================================================
# INTERPOLATION DISPATCH REGISTRY
# ============================================================

# Define the strict signature every curve kernel must follow.
# Signature: (tenors, values, targets, out) -> None
InterpKernelFn = Callable[[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray], None]

# Map (Interp Method ID, Extrap Method ID) -> Math Kernel
# We cast the np.int8 enums to pure Python ints for safe, fast dictionary hashing.
CURVE_MATH_REGISTRY: dict[tuple[int, int], InterpKernelFn] = {
    (
        int(INTERP_LINEAR),
        int(EXTRAP_FLAT),
    ): interp_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    (
        int(INTERP_LINEAR),
        int(EXTRAP_LINEAR),
    ): interp_linear_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    (
        int(INTERP_LOG_LINEAR),
        int(EXTRAP_FLAT),
    ): interp_log_linear_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    (
        int(INTERP_LOG_LINEAR),
        int(EXTRAP_LINEAR),
    ): interp_log_linear_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    (
        int(INTERP_STEP_LEFT),
        int(EXTRAP_FLAT),
    ): interp_step_left_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    (
        int(INTERP_STEP_LEFT),
        int(EXTRAP_LINEAR),
    ): interp_step_left_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    (
        int(INTERP_STEP_RIGHT),
        int(EXTRAP_FLAT),
    ): interp_step_right_flat_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
    (
        int(INTERP_STEP_RIGHT),
        int(EXTRAP_LINEAR),
    ): interp_step_right_linear_extrap_3d_numba_kernel,
}

```python
# src\riskquant\market_data\curves\interp_methods.py

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from enum import IntEnum

import numpy as np


class InterpMethod(IntEnum):
    LINEAR = 1
    LOG_LINEAR = 2
    STEP_LEFT = 3
    STEP_RIGHT = 4
    CUBIC_SPLINE = 5


# compiler-friendly int8 constants
INTERP_LINEAR: np.int8 = np.int8(InterpMethod.LINEAR)
INTERP_LOG_LINEAR: np.int8 = np.int8(InterpMethod.LOG_LINEAR)
INTERP_STEP_LEFT: np.int8 = np.int8(InterpMethod.STEP_LEFT)
INTERP_STEP_RIGHT: np.int8 = np.int8(InterpMethod.STEP_RIGHT)
INTERP_CUBIC_SPLINE: np.int8 = np.int8(InterpMethod.CUBIC_SPLINE)

# Interp methods
INTERP_METHOD_NAMES: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(
    method.name.upper() for method in InterpMethod
)

# Central registry
INTERP_METHOD_REGISTRY: dict[str, InterpMethod] = {
    "LINEAR": InterpMethod.LINEAR,
    "LOG_LINEAR": InterpMethod.LOG_LINEAR,
    "STEP_LEFT": InterpMethod.STEP_LEFT,
    "STEP_RIGHT": InterpMethod.STEP_RIGHT,
    "CUBIC_SPLINE": InterpMethod.CUBIC_SPLINE,
}


def validate_interpolation_methods_dict(
    interpolation_methods_dict: Mapping[str, int],
) -> None:
    expected = {name: int(member) for name, member in InterpMethod.__members__.items()}
    got = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in interpolation_methods_dict.items()}

    if got == expected:
        return

    missing = {k: expected[k] for k in expected.keys() - got.keys()}
    extra = {k: got[k] for k in got.keys() - expected.keys()}
    different = {
        k: {"expected": expected[k], "got": got[k]}
        for k in (expected.keys() & got.keys())
        if expected[k] != got[k]
    }

    raise ValueError(
        "interpolation_methods sheet does not match riskquant InterpolationMethod enum.\n"
        f"missing={missing}\n"
        f"extra={extra}\n"
        f"different={different}\n"
        "Fix the Excel interpolation_methods table or update riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_methods.InterpMethod."
    )
```
